  Dobrý deň, pán Slávik,                                                        
                                                                                
  ďakujem za Vašu prihlášku na pozíciu Junior AI Developer. Rád Vám oznamujem,  
  že postupujete do ďalšieho kola výberového konania. Jeho súčasťou je          
  praktická úloha, ktorá nám ukáže, ako pristupujete k problému a ako pracujete 
  s AI. Pripravil som dve zadania, vyberte si jedno z nich.                     
                                                                                
  Pracovať môžete v Claude Code alebo v podobnom nástroji. Ak nestihnete        
  všetko, uprednostnite podstatné časti a uveďte, čo v riešení chýba. V README  
  prosím popíšte, ako ste s AI pracovali (aké pokyny dostali agenti, ako ste    
  kontrolovali ich výstup a kde sa pomýlili). Ak Vám v zadaní niečo nebude      
  jasné, rozhodnite podľa seba a v README zdôvodnite, prečo ste sa tak          
  rozhodli. S otázkami sa na mňa môžete kedykoľvek obrátiť aj odpoveďou na      
  tento e-mail.                                                                 
                                                                                
  ZADANIE A: Spoľahlivá databáza investorov                                     
                                                                                
  Úloha sa týka databázy investorov do firiem: VC fondov, private equity,       
  family office, angel investorov a veľkých súkromných investorov. Kľúčová je   
  spoľahlivosť dát: každý záznam musí byť skutočný investor, nie náhodná firma. 
  Pri každom investorovi musí byť uvedené, v akom sektore pôsobí, do čoho       
  bežne investuje a v akej výške, aký má celkový investičný kapitál a odkiaľ    
  tieto informácie pochádzajú.                                                  
                                                                                
  Začnite plánom. Z neho musí byť jasné, kto v databáze bude a kto nie, teda    
  podľa akých pravidiel subjekt zaradíte alebo vyradíte. Popíšte tiež, ako      
  overíte pravosť a správne zaradenie záznamov. Odhadnite, aký okruh investorov 
  je možné z verejných zdrojov získať, približne koľko ich bude a aká bude      
  spoľahlivosť dát. Uveďte, z čoho odhad vychádza.                              
                                                                                
  Plán následne overte na menšej vzorke, napríklad na VC fondoch v jednej       
  krajine. Pri každom zázname uveďte zdroj a dátum, ktoré dokladajú, že subjekt 
  skutočne investuje. Na ručne overenej vzorke zmerajte presnosť. Na záver      
  odhadnite náklady na rozšírenie na celý svet.                                 
                                                                                
  Používajte výhradne verejne dostupné dáta.                                    
                                                                                
  ZADANIE B: Konverzná landing page                                             
                                                                                
  Cieľom je landing page s porovnaním ETF obchodovaných na NYSE pre českého     
  drobného investora, ktorý prichádza z reklamy na mobile a stránke venuje len  
  niekoľko sekúnd. Stránka má fungovať ako lead magnet: ponúknuť návštevníkovi  
  hodnotu a výmenou za ňu získať jeho kontakt. Pekný dizajn nestačí, stránka    
  musí byť optimalizovaná na prínos kontaktov. Zároveň nesmie pôsobiť ako       
  základný výstup od Claude. Dizajn aj texty majú byť premyslené pre túto       
  cieľovú skupinu. Pri hodnotení sa budem riadiť hlavne otázkou, či by sme na   
  ňu mohli zajtra spustiť reklamu.                                              
                                                                                
  Určte cieľovú skupinu, čo návštevník dostane výmenou za kontakt a v ktorom    
  momente oň požiadate. Hodnota by mala byť zrejmá ešte pred vyplnením          
  formulára. Navrhnite aj dve verzie reklamy. Prvá obrazovka stránky by mala    
  nadväzovať na prísľub reklamy a okamžite komunikovať, čo návštevník získa.    
                                                                                
  Výber interaktívnych prvkov, napríklad porovnania fondov, kalkulačky          
  poplatkov alebo skladania portfólia, nechávam na Vás. Každý z nich by mal     
  viesť ku kontaktu. Formulár by mal mať čo najmenej polí. Premyslite aj to, čo 
  návštevník dostane po jeho odoslaní.                                          
                                                                                
  Použite skutočné dáta s uvedením zdroja a merajte celú cestu od príchodu po   
  odoslanie formulára. V README zdôvodnite poradie sekcií, uveďte očakávanú     
  konverziu a tri hypotézy na A/B test zoradené podľa očakávaného dopadu.       
  Stránku nasaďte na bezplatný hosting, napríklad Vercel.                       
                                                                                
  Riešenie prosím zašlite do 30. 10. 2026 ako odpoveď na tento e-mail. Priložte 
  odkaz na verejný repozitár na GitHube a pri zadaní B aj URL stránky. V        
  repozitári by mal byť výsledok (pri zadaní A plán, dáta zo vzorky so zdrojom  
  pri každom údaji a výsledok merania presnosti, pri zadaní B obe verzie        
  reklamy), kód s priebežnou históriou commitov a v priečinku ai-log export     
  konverzácií s Claude Code (príkaz /export).                                   
                                                                                
  Teším sa na Vaše riešenie.

     ## 1. Kľúčový insight (na ňom stojí celá stránka)

     SCHD…) u bežného

⏺ Plán som spísal a commitol do repozitára (PLAN.md, commit 6583d01). Ešte som
  nič neimplementoval ani neoveroval.

  Na čom stojí: český drobný investor si VOO, SPY, VTI a ďalšie ETF z NYSE u
  XTB, Trading 212 či Revolutu väčšinou nekúpi. Od roku 2018 na to EÚ brokeri
  pre retail klientov potrebujú dokument KID (regulácia PRIIPs) a americké ETF
  ho nemajú. Stránka preto ku každému americkému ETF ukáže európske „dvojča“
  (UCITS fond), ktoré si v ČR kúpiť dá, a porovná poplatky. Návštevník tak
  dostane užitočnú informáciu hneď po kliknutí na ticker, ešte pred formulárom.
  Plná tabuľka s poznámkami k daniam a brokerom potom príde za e-mail, pričom
  formulár má len jedno pole.

  Druhá reklama ide cez peniaze: „rovnaký S&P 500, o X % ročne lacnejšie, za 20
  rokov rozdiel Y Kč“. Stránka bude v češtine, README v slovenčine.

  Treba rozhodnúť, kým pokračujem:
  1. Hlavná myšlienka: súhlasíš s uhlom „toto ETF si v ČR nekúpiš, tu je jeho
     dvojča“? Celá stránka je postavená na ňom. Zadanie však hovorí o ETF z
     NYSE, takže sa môže stať, že Matej chcel jednoduché porovnanie amerických
     fondov. QQQ sa navyše obchoduje na Nasdaqu, nie na NYSE, takže ho buď
     vynechám, alebo uvediem s poznámkou.
  2. Meranie cesty: odporúčam PostHog, ktorý funnely zobrazuje bez ďalšieho
     nastavovania. Potrebuješ účet a projektový kľúč. Alternatívou je vlastný
     endpoint na Verceli s ukladaním do Upstash Redis.
  3. Leady a e-maily: Upstash na ukladanie kontaktov a Resend na odosielanie
     (oba majú free tarif). Potrebuješ si v nich založiť účty. Bez nich sa
     výsledok po odoslaní zobrazí len na stránke a e-mail nepríde.
  4. Vercel: CLI nie je nainštalované. Spusť npm i -g vercel a vercel login,
     prípadne ťa tým prevediem pri nasadzovaní.

  Keď plán schváliš alebo upravíš, začnem krokom 2. Agenti paralelne zozbierajú
  dáta o poplatkoch od emitentov fondov (zdroj a dátum pri každom údaji) a ďalší
  agent ich krížovo overí.

  Na konci nezabudni spustiť /export do priečinka ai-log/. Tento rozhovor má byť
  jeho prvou časťou.

❯ rob commity ale ich este nepushuj prosim ale cele to implementuj a potom to   
  pretestujem, čo najlepší dizajn a UX,                                         
                                                                                
  takto vyzera ich stranka a tiez vidno tam obrazok do ich software:            

⏺ Začínam. Postup bude takýto:

  1. Dáta paralelne cez agentov: jeden agent zbiera americké ETF od emitentov,
     druhý ich UCITS dvojčatá, tretí české pravidlá (PRIIPs, dane, poplatky
     bankových fondov). Medzitým ja staviam stránku.
  2. Vlastná značka: stránka nebude používať značku Clientela, pôjde o
     samostatný investičný portál, aké pre klientov stavajú. Smer dizajnu však
     prevezmem od nich: serifový nadpis, tmavá zelenomodrá a tehlovo červená.
  3. Bez účtov (PostHog, Upstash, Resend) všetko pobeží v záložnom režime. Kľúče
     sa doplnia len cez env premenné.

   ├ Research UCITS twin ETFs
   └ Research Czech rules and fund fees

⏺ Bash(mkdir -p ~/clientelo-task/src ~/clientelo-task/tests && cd
      ~/clientelo-task && cat > src/calc.js <<'EOF'
      /**…)
  ⎿  ℹ todo 0
     ℹ duration_ms 47.608833
     … +5 lines (ctrl+o to expand)
  ⎿  Created clientelo-task/src/calc.js (+29 -0)
       0 +/**                                                            
       1 + * Budoucí hodnota pravidelné měsíční investice po odečtení    
         +nákladů.                                                       
       2 + * - annualReturn: hrubý roční výnos trhu (např. 0.07)         
       3 + * - terPct: roční průběžné náklady fondu v % (TER), strhávají 
         + se z majetku                                                  
       4 + * - entryFeePct: vstupní poplatek v % z každého vkladu        
       5 + */                                                            
       6 +export function futureValue({ monthly, years, annualReturn,    
         +terPct = 0, entryFeePct = 0 }) {                               
       7 +  const netAnnual = (1 + annualReturn) * (1 - terPct / 100) -  
         +1;                                                             
       8 +  const r = Math.pow(1 + netAnnual, 1 / 12) - 1;               
       9 +  const invested = monthly * (1 - entryFeePct / 100);          
      10 +  let value = 0;                                               
      11 +  for (let i = 0; i < years * 12; i++) value = (value +        
         +invested) * (1 + r);                                           
      12 +  return value;                                                
      13 +}                                                              
      14 +                                                               
      15 +/** Kolik investora stojí náklady fondu = rozdíl proti stejné  
         +investici bez nákladů (včetně ušlého zhodnocení). */           
      16 +export function feeCost(opts) {                                
      17 +  return futureValue({ ...opts, terPct: 0, entryFeePct: 0 }) - 
         + futureValue(opts);                                            
      18 +}                                                              
      19 +                                                               
      20 +export function compare({ monthly, years, annualReturn, bank,  
         +etf }) {                                                       
      21 +  const bankCost = feeCost({ monthly, years, annualReturn,     
         +terPct: bank.ter_pct, entryFeePct: bank.entry_fee_pct });

  **Surprises**
  - **Vanguard ex-US funds are new.** Vanguard launched FTSE All-World ex-US
  UCITS funds on 2026-08-18. The Acc class trades on Xetra under the ticker
  "VXUS" in EUR, so investors could confuse it with the US fund. The Dist class
  is VXUD. Both charge 0.12%. They are very small: about EUR 127 m (Acc) and EUR
  10 m (Dist) per justETF.
  - **VWCE got cheaper.** Its TER is now 0.14%, down from 0.19% from 2026-07-28.
  justETF shows 0.14%. The date and the earlier 0.22% to 0.19% cut come from
  German press found by web search; I did not check a Vanguard document.
  - **SPYL is the cheapest S&P 500 option.** It is 0.03% against 0.07% for VUAA
  and CSPX. State Street's site calls it "State Street® SPDR® S&P® 500 UCITS ETF
  (Acc)" with ticker SPYL (primary listing on Deutsche Börse). The "SPY5"
  ticker you mentioned did not appear on the fetched pages.
  - **No close UCITS twin exists for several funds.** None of my searches found
  a UCITS fund on CRSP US Total Market (VTI), Dow Jones U.S. Dividend 100
  (SCHD), S&P U.S. Dividend Growers (VIG) or CRSP US Large Cap Growth (VUG). The
  twins listed for those use a different index.
  - **IUAA has no Xetra listing** on justETF. The only EUR listing is gettex
  (IS03).
  - **The ISIN IE00B3F81409 is not IUAA.** It belongs to iShares Core Global
  Aggregate Bond (AGGG), a different fund. Don't use it for BND.

  **Conflicts**
  1. **SPYL fund size.** State Street gives total fund assets of USD 45,049 m
  (21 Oct 2026). justETF gives EUR 17,401 m. justETF most likely shows only this
  share class, while State Street counts the whole fund, which also has a Dist
  class. Read all justETF sizes in this list as share-class sizes, not fund
  totals.
  2. **VXUS fund size.** Vanguard's page says total fund assets are USD 117.15 m
  (undated). justETF gives EUR 127 m for the Acc class alone. These don't
  reconcile cleanly.

  **Unverifiable or not done**
  - **No as-of date on justETF profile sizes.** Those sizes carry my access date
  (2026-10-22). Exceptions: XD9U and EXUS sizes come from justETF index lists
  dated 2026-10-21, and SPYL's issuer AUM is dated 2026-10-21.
  - **Few issuer cross-checks.** Only SPYL was checked against a full issuer
  page. For CSPX, the iShares page was cut off before the TER and assets.
  Vanguard's issuer pages were not fetched (Vanguard ex-US was checked on its NL
  site). Everything else rests on justETF alone.
  - **Small-cap coverage of US VXUS.** Its index (FTSE Global All Cap ex US,
  which includes small caps) is from general knowledge, not a fetched page.
  - **Xetra-Gold costs beyond the 0.00% TER** were not checked.
  - **Not checked:** Dist share classes VFEM and EQQQ (IE0032077012), and the
  iShares DJ US Select Dividend (DE) fund.
  - **PRIIPs KID availability in the Czech Republic** was not checked for any
  fund. justETF warns that VUAA and VXUS may not be available in every country
  of residence, and its notes on the other pages weren't checked. Since the KID
  is the whole point, confirm each one at the Czech broker before relying on
  this list.

⏺ Agent "Research UCITS twin ETFs" finished · 4m 11s

⏺ Agent pre UCITS dvojčatá skončil, no s jednou chybou: VXUS je nesprávne
  označený ako „exact“ napriek inému indexu podľa vlastnej poznámky. Opravím to
  a zapíšem do README, popri tom dokončím API.

› Message from general-purpose
  ## Summary

  I verified all 15 ETFs. Most values come from the issuers' own pages and data:
  - **Vanguard:** the advisors.vanguard.com fund pages (their embedded
  structured data) plus Vanguard's own JSON API at
  investor.vanguard.com/vmf/api/{TICKER}/…, which feeds the investor fund pages.
  - **iShares:** the ishares.com fund pages (structured data).
  - **SPDR (SPY, GLD):** the ssga.com fund pages.

  Two issuers blocked fetching, so I used stockanalysis.com as a fallback where
  noted:
  - **Schwab (SCHD):** schwabassetmanagement.com returned HTTP 403 on every
  attempt.
  - **Invesco (QQQ):** the product-detail page is rendered by JavaScript and has
  no fund data in it, and its API returned HTTP 406.

  Vanguard pages never show the listing exchange, so the exchange for all
  Vanguard ETFs comes from stockanalysis.com.

  I wrote no files to the project. The only files are temporary downloads in
  /private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-simon-slavik/32e6725e-8b24-4d43-b027-c1119f874d
  18/scratchpad/.

  ```json
  2026-06-30","accessed":"2026-10-22"},{"field":"exchange,aum_usd,distribution
  (FALLBACK)","url":"https://stockanalysis.com/etf/qqq/","as_of":null,"accessed"
  :"2026-10-22"}]}
  ]
  ```

  ## What I could not verify

  - **SCHD:**
    - The 10-year NAV return is null because the Schwab site was blocked.
    - Every other SCHD value comes only from stockanalysis.com: AUM $114.79B,
  0.06%, NYSE Arca, inception 2011-10-20, quarterly, Dow Jones U.S. Dividend
  100. The AUM has no date on the page.
    - A web-search snippet quoted Schwab's page as showing $116.3B as of
  2026-10-20. I could not open that page, so I did not use it.
  - **QQQ:**
    - AUM ($485.34B, no date), exchange and dividend frequency come only from
  stockanalysis.com.
    - Invesco's page shows no AUM, exchange or distribution frequency.
    - The 10-year figure of 22.06% is as of 2026-06-30, not 2026-09-30.
  Invesco's wording is "QQQ NAV 10-year performance reflected 22.06% growth
  versus 15.48% by the S&P 500" and it does not literally say "annualized". The
  S&P comparison suggests it is annualized, but confirm before publishing.
  - **Exchange for the Vanguard ETFs** comes only from stockanalysis.com,
  because Vanguard's pages don't show it.
  - **IWM and IVV returns:** iShares labels the 10-year row "Total Return (%)"
  and puts it above a separate "Market Price (%)" row, so I read it as the NAV
  return. That's an inference, not an explicit "NAV" label.
  - **GLD distribution:** SSGA's page has no explicit field for this. "No
  distributions" is based on the page saying GLD "does not generate any income"
  and sells gold to pay its expenses.

  ## Conflicts between sources

  - **Vanguard AUM:** the figures in the JSON are the ETF share class only, as
  shown on Vanguard's pages ("Net assets USD 1032.3B" for VOO). The whole fund
  across all share classes is much larger: VOO $1.7T, VTI $2.3T, VT $101.2B,
  VXUS $649.3B, VEA $313.5B, VWO $164.7B, VUG $392.2B, VIG $127.7B, BND $389.3B,
  all as of 2026-09-30.
  - **BND AUM:** stockanalysis.com shows $164.32B with no date, against
  Vanguard's $161.4B as of 2026-09-30. The gap is probably just the different
  dates.
  - **QQQ name:** Invesco calls it "Invesco QQQ ETF", while stockanalysis.com
  still shows "Invesco QQQ Trust Series I".
  - **AUM dates are mixed:** SSGA and iShares show 2026-10-21, while Vanguard is
  month-end 2026-09-30. Ranking the funds by size on the page mixes those
  dates.

  ## Surprising findings

  - **Two of the 15 Vanguard-family ETFs are not on NYSE Arca:** VXUS and BND
  are listed on Nasdaq. QQQ is also Nasdaq-listed, as you expected. If you want
  only NYSE-listed funds, drop VXUS, BND and QQQ; the other 12 are on NYSE Arca.
  - **VTI and VUG have new names and benchmarks:** "Vanguard Morningstar Total
  Stock Market ETF" (Morningstar U.S. Total Market Index) and "Vanguard
  Morningstar Growth ETF" (Morningstar U.S. Large Cap Growth Index). This is the
  renaming of the CRSP indexes after Morningstar bought CRSP, effective
  2026-07-29. Vanguard's notice says only the names change, not the index
  construction. Older copy saying "CRSP" is now out of date.
  - **QQQ changed structure:** it converted from a trust to an open-end ETF in
  December 2025, and its expense ratio fell from 0.20% to 0.18% (per a web
  search pointing to etf.com and advisorperspectives.com; I did not open those
  pages). The Invesco page confirms 0.18%.
  - **SPY's fee is 0.0945%,** about three times that of VOO and IVV (0.03%), and
  its 10-year return is correspondingly a little lower: 15.18% against 15.29%
  for VOO and 15.30% for IVV. SSGA's page now calls it "State Street SPDR S&P
  500 ETF Trust".
  - **IVV ($890.4B as of 10/21) is bigger than SPY ($820.7B as of 10/21).**
  VOO's ETF share class is $1,032.3B as of 9/30.
  - **VXUS, VWO and VT carry their reduced fees** of 0.05%, 0.06% and 0.06%,
  with "as of" dates of 2026-02-27.
  - **Note for Czech/EU readers:** every one of these funds distributes. None is
  accumulating, and none offers an accumulating share class. These US-domiciled
  ETFs also generally have no PRIIPs KID, so EU retail brokers often can't sell
  them to retail clients. That point comes from general knowledge, not from a
  page I fetched, so it's worth stating carefully on the landing page.

⏺ Agent "Research US NYSE ETF data" finished · 5m 24s

⏺ VXUS, BND a QQQ sa obchodujú na Nasdaqu, nie na NYSE, takže ich vyraďujem a
  zostáva 12 ETF z NYSE Arca – zdôvodnenie zapíšem do README. Pri VWO použijem
  náhradu VFEA od Vanguardu a EIMI pridám ako alternatívu, teraz zostavujem
  dátový súbor.

› Message from general-purpose
  # Fact-check: Czech ETF landing page (researched 2026-10-22)

  All "accessed" dates below are 2026-10-22. EUR-Lex itself returned an empty
  page or 403 to both WebFetch and curl. I therefore read the official
  Regulation and Directive texts through the EU Publications Office CELEX
  resource, which serves the same Official Journal text. Several broker sites
  block automated access (Trading 212 pricing, DEGIRO, Revolut, Portu), so some
  broker points below are weaker. They are flagged.

  Two of the brief's assumptions are wrong:
  - **The 40 mil. Kč cap no longer applies to securities.** It applied to
  securities only in tax year 2025 and was abolished from 1 January 2026. Today
  the statute applies a 40 mil. Kč cap only to crypto-assets.
  - **The paragraph letters are t) and u).** The 100 000 Kč exemption is § 4
  odst. 1 písm. t), and the 3-year time test is písm. u).

  ---

  ## 1. PRIIPs / KID

  **1a. A KID is mandatory before a PRIIP is offered to retail investors**
  - **Claim:** Art. 5(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1286/2014: "Before a PRIIP is
  made available to retail investors, the PRIIP manufacturer shall draw up for
  that product a key information document…" Art. 13(1): "A person advising on,
  or selling, a PRIIP shall provide retail investors with the key information
  document in good time before those retail investors are bound by any
  contract…" Art. 4(6) defines a retail investor by reference to a MiFID II
  retail client.
  - **Czech:** „Podle nařízení EU č. 1286/2014 (PRIIPs) smí broker prodat
  retailovému investorovi investiční produkt jen tehdy, když k němu existuje
  sdělení klíčových informací (KID).“
  - **Source:** Official text (OJ L 352, 9.12.2014) fetched from
  https://publications.europa.eu/resource/celex/32014R1286. Canonical citation
  link: https://eur-lex.europa.eu/eli/reg/2014/1286/oj
  - **Confidence:** High

  **1b. That is why EU brokers don't sell US-domiciled ETFs (VOO, SPY…) to
  retail clients**
  - **Claim:** The broker Lynx says the KID is mandatory under EU law, that
  retail investors are "not permitted to trade ETFs or other products that do
  not have a valid KID", and that "Retail clients cannot purchase ETFs from
  countries where the issuer does not publish a KID". It lists US ETFs among the
  restricted products. Existing positions can be held or sold, but not added
  to. The KID obligation has applied since 1 January 2018, and to UCITS funds
  since 1 January 2023.
  - **Czech:** „Americké ETF (např. VOO, SPY) nemají evropský KID, a proto je
  evropští brokeři retailovým klientům neprodají. Už držené pozice lze prodat,
  ale nelze je navyšovat.“
  - **Source:** https://www.lynxbroker.com/service/products-and-regulations/regu
  lations/key-information-document/ (page undated; accessed 2026-10-22)
  - **Supporting source:** A search snippet of IBKR's PRIIPs page
  (https://ibkb.interactivebrokers.com/node/2993, which now redirects to
  ibkr.info/node/2993) says: "As a broker, IBKR is required to block trading in
  a PRIIP if a KID is not available." The page itself returned 403 or a
  JavaScript-only shell, so I could not fetch it directly.
  - **Confidence:** High for the mechanism (Lynx page fetched). Medium for the
  IBKR quote.

  **1c. Option: being treated as a professional client on request**
  - **Claim:** MiFID II (Directive 2014/65/EU), Annex II, section II.1: a retail
  client may ask in writing to be treated as professional. The firm must assess
  them, and at least two of these three criteria must be met:
    - on average 10 significant-size transactions per quarter over the previous
  four quarters;
    - a portfolio (cash plus financial instruments) above EUR 500 000;
    - at least one year working in the financial sector in a professional
  position.

    The client then gives up retail protections. Lynx states that the restricted
  products "can only be traded by professional clients under MiFID II."
  - **Czech:** „Výjimkou je status profesionálního klienta na žádost. Podle
  MiFID II musíte splnit aspoň 2 ze 3 podmínek, např. portfolio nad 500 000 EUR,
  a přicházíte tím o část ochrany retailového investora.“
  - **Sources:** https://publications.europa.eu/resource/celex/32014L0065 (Annex
  II) and the Lynx page above.
  - **Confidence:** High

  ---

  ## 2. Czech tax on selling ETFs (zákon č. 586/1992 Sb.)

  The statute was checked on zakonyprolidi.cz, which shows "verze 157, aktuální
  znění platné od 01.08.2026 do 31.12.2026":
  https://www.zakonyprolidi.cz/cs/1992-586

  **2a. 100 000 Kč annual exemption: § 4 odst. 1 písm. t)**
  - **Claim:** The exempt income is "příjmy z úplatného převodu cenných papírů,
  pokud jejich úhrn u poplatníka nepřesáhne ve zdaňovacím období částku 100000
  Kč". This is the total gross proceeds, not the profit. It also covers
  fund-unit payouts on dissolution of a unit fund. Securities held in business
  assets are excluded.
  - **Czech:** „Pokud vaše celkové příjmy z prodeje cenných papírů za rok
  nepřesáhnou 100 000 Kč (počítá se hrubá částka prodejů, ne zisk), jsou od daně
  osvobozeny.“
  - **Confidence:** High

  **2b. 3-year time test: § 4 odst. 1 písm. u)**
  - **Claim:** The exempt income is "příjem z úplatného převodu cenného papíru,
  přesáhne-li doba mezi nabytím a úplatným převodem…" 3 years. The period is 5
  years for a kmenový list. There are exclusions for business assets within 3
  years after business activity ends, and for qualifying employee options.
  - **Czech:** „Když ETF držíte déle než 3 roky, zisk z prodeje je osvobozen od
  daně (časový test).“
  - **Confidence:** High

  **2c. 40 mil. Kč cap**
  - **Claim:**
    - From 1 January 2025 a cap of 40 mil. Kč per taxpayer per year applied to
  exempt income from selling securities and business shares.
    - The Chamber of Deputies approved the Senate amendments abolishing it on 10
  September 2025. The cap is removed from 1 January 2026, and 2025 sales stay
  under the old rules.
    - In the current statute, 40 000 000 Kč appears only in § 4 odst. 3, and
  only for crypto-assets under § 4 odst. 1 písm. zk). It no longer applies to
  securities.
  - **Czech:** „Od roku 2026 už pro cenné papíry neplatí žádný strop pro
  osvobození po 3 letech (limit 40 mil. Kč se uplatnil jen u prodejů v roce
  2025).“ Alternatively, drop the cap from the page entirely.
  - **Sources:**
    - zakonyprolidi.cz (above): the current § 4 odst. 3 text applies only to
  crypto-assets.
    - https://portal.pohoda.cz/rychle-zpravy/nove-zdaneni-prijmu-z-prodeje-cenny
  ch-papiru-od-2026/ (14. 10. 2025)
    - The search snippet also cites https://www.e15.cz/finexpert/danove-priznani
  /od-ledna-plati-novy-limit-pro-zdaneni-prijmu-z-obchodovani-s-cennymi-papiry-a
  -podily-v-obchodnich-korporacich-1423019 and
  https://www.forvismazars.com/cz/cs/prehledy/newslettery/newslettery-z-danove-o
  blasti/forvis-mazars-tax-view-archiv/omezeni-osvobozeni-prijmu-z-prodeje (2025
  introduction).
  - **Confidence:** High that the current statute has no securities cap. Medium
  on the exact repeal mechanics: I did not identify the Sb. number of the
  amending law, and Crowe (18. 11. 2025, page returned 403) reportedly calls the
  removal "partial".

  ---

  ## 3. Dividends and withholding tax

  **3a. US–CZ treaty: 15% on portfolio dividends**
  - **Claim:** The 1993 US–Czech treaty, Art. 10(2), caps source-country tax on
  dividends at 5% for a company holding at least 10%, and 15% on other
  dividends. The IRS Technical Explanation says: "The maximum tax at source on
  dividends is 5 percent on dividends paid by a 10 percent-owned subsidiary to
  its parent corporation, and 15 percent on other dividends."
  - **Czech:** „U amerických ETF (např. VOO) vám USA strhnou z dividend 15 %
  daně, pokud máte u brokera podepsaný formulář W-8BEN (jinak 30 %).“
  - **Sources:**
    - https://www.irs.gov/pub/irs-trty/czech.pdf (treaty text extracted from the
  PDF)
    - https://www.irs.gov/businesses/international-businesses/czech-republic-tax
  -treaty-documents (last reviewed 09-Aug-2026)
  - **Confidence:** High for the 15% treaty rate.

  **3b. W-8BEN is how you claim the treaty rate**
  - **Claim:** The IRS W-8BEN instructions say the form is used to "claim a
  reduced rate of, or exemption from, withholding as a resident of a foreign
  country" (Rev. 10/2021). XTB's help pages describe 15% instead of 30% with
  W-8BEN, depending on the treaty. I saw XTB only in a search snippet and did
  not fetch it.
  - **Sources:** https://www.irs.gov/instructions/iw8ben (last reviewed
  30-Apr-2026); https://www.xtb.com/en/help-center/tax-statement-5/w-8ben
  (snippet only)
  - **Confidence:** High for the IRS part. The 30% default is the US statutory
  rate; I saw it only in third-party sources, not on an IRS page fetched here
  (medium).

  **3c. Czech side for directly received foreign dividends**
  - **Claim:** Foreign dividends must be declared in the Czech tax return under
  § 8, with a credit for foreign tax (§ 38f, Příloha 3).
  - **Source:**
  https://www.finmag.cz/finance/309218-investice-a-dane-ii-dividendy (2016,
  outdated). No official Finanční správa page was fetched.
  - **Confidence:** Medium. I did not verify the current rates or filing
  thresholds from the statute.

  **3d. Irish-domiciled accumulating UCITS ETFs**
  - **Claim:** Under the US–Ireland treaty the Irish fund pays 15% US
  withholding internally. An accumulating fund reinvests dividends, so the
  investor has no annual dividend income to declare. Finzi.cz says "Daňová
  smlouva USA–Irsko snižuje tuto sazbu pro kvalifikované fondy na 15 %";
  accumulating funds "reinvestují dividendy uvnitř fondu", and the investor
  "neřeší ročně dividendový příjem v daňovém přiznání".
  - **Czech:** „Irské akumulační ETF (např. CSPX, VWCE) dividendy automaticky
  reinvestují. 15% americkou daň zaplatí fond sám a vy dividendy v daňovém
  přiznání neřešíte.“
  - **Sources:**
    - https://www.finzi.cz/etf/clanky/ireland-domiciled-etf-proc-jsou-vyhodne/
  (Jan Pospíšil, updated 31. 5. 2026)
    - Corroborating search snippets:
  https://www.inflace.cz/akumulacni-distribucni-etf/,
  https://taxero.cz/blog/musim-platit-dan-z-dividend-z-etf-v-cesku (not fetched)
  - **Confidence:** Medium. These are reputable but secondary sources. I found
  no Finanční správa statement or text of the US–Ireland treaty.

  ---

  to be KBC's internal code, not a CZ ISIN) | **3,00 %** | **2,59 % p.a.**
  "poplatky za správu a jiné správní nebo provozní náklady" (an estimate), plus

  poplatek 3–4,5 % a roční náklady kolem 1,8–2,6 %. Indexové ETF stojí často jen
  0,07–0,22 % ročně.“ The 0,07 % TER for CSPX/VUAA comes from justETF and
  lightyear snippets, and the 0,22 % for VWCE from TradingView. I did not fetch
  the issuer pages, so treat these TERs as medium confidence.

  ## 5. Brokers: UCITS ETF availability and headline fees

  **XTB**
  - **Availability:** Instrument pages exist and were fetched (these are XTB's
  /en pages):
    - CSPX.UK "iShares Core S&P 500 UCITS ETF (Acc USD)":
  https://www.xtb.com/en/trading-services/range-of-markets/etfs-trading/cspx-uk
    - SXR8.DE: …/sxr8-de
    - VUAA.DE "Vanguard S&P 500 UCITS ETF USD (Acc, EUR)": …/vuaa-de
    - VWCE.DE "Vanguard FTSE All-World UCITS ETF (Acc EUR)": …/vwce-de
  - **Czech fees:** On https://www.xtb.com/cz/ucet-a-poplatky, stocks and ETFs
  are "Zdarma… Při měsíčním obj. do 100 000 EUR (pak popl. 0,2 %, min. 10 EUR)".
  The currency conversion fee ("Poplatek za konverzi měn") is 0,5 %.
  https://www.xtb.com/cz/etf says "více než 2300 různých ETF" and names the
  providers iShares, Vanguard and Amundi.
  - **Confidence:** High

  **Trading 212**
  - **Fees:** The help centre article "What are the fees in the Invest, ISAs,
  and SIPP?" says: "FX fee: 0.15% · Trading commission: Free · Custody Fee:
  Free". https://helpcentre.trading212.com/hc/en-us/articles/11471996799517-What
  -are-the-fees-in-the-Invest-ISAs-and-SIPP (accessed 2026-10-22). Confidence:
  high.
  - **Availability:** Not verified from an official page; trading212.com
  returned 403. A Trading 212 community thread from 29. 12. 2020 discusses VUAA
  on the platform
  (https://community.trading212.com/t/vanguard-vusa-or-vuaa/33762). A Polish
  guide (freenance.io) mentions VWCE and CSPX. Confidence: low.

  **DEGIRO**
  - **Not verified.** Every degiro.* page returned 503. Third-party sources
  (quasa.io, finorum.com, curvo.eu) say Core Selection ETFs cost a 1 EUR
  handling fee, and only on Tradegate since October 2025. Confidence: low. Do
  not publish without checking degiro.cz yourself.

  **Interactive Brokers**
  - **Fees:**
  https://www.interactivebrokers.com/en/pricing/commissions-stocks-europe.php
  (fetched). Germany: Fixed (SmartRouting) 0,05 % of trade value, min EUR 3,00;
  Tiered 0,05 %, min EUR 1,25 plus third-party fees. UK: Fixed 0,05 %, min GBP 3
  / EUR 3 / USD 4. Confidence: high.
  - **Availability of CSPX/VUAA/VWCE:** No fetched page confirmed it. Not
  verified.

  **Fio (e-Broker / Smartbroker)**
  - **Fees:** https://www2.fio.cz/stocks-investments/stocks/stocks-germany, with
  a new structure effective from February 2026. German market:
    - up to €500 on DIP accounts: €1,98
    - up to €1 400: €3,95
    - €1 400–3 000: €4,95
    - over €3 000: 0,15 %, min €6,95
    - custody free
  - **Availability:** The page mentions "over 3,000 ETF/ETP instruments" but no
  specific tickers. CSPX/VUAA/VWCE not verified.
  - **Confidence:** Medium-high on fees; low on specific ETFs.

  **Revolut**
  - **Fees:** Revolut's own pages returned 403 or a security check. The press
  release of 17. 1. 2025 (https://www.e15.cz/tiskove-zpravy/revolut-oznamil-spus
  teni-etf-investicnich-planu-bez-poplatku-1421634) says commission-free
  investment plans in "více než 300 ETF kotovaných v EU" from issuers including
  BlackRock, Vanguard and Amundi, available "v celé EHP, včetně České
  republiky".
  - **Unverified:** A search snippet of Revolut's CZ help page mentions 25 Kč
  per order outside the plan's allowance; I could not fetch the page.
  - **Availability:** Specific tickers not verified.
  - **Confidence:** Medium (press release only).

  **Portu**
  - **Fees:** portu.cz is a JavaScript-only page and could not be read. The
  finex.cz review (updated 14. 10. 2026, https://finex.cz/recenze/portu/) gives:
    - management fee "Od 0,24 % ročně" (Platinum level with a 15-year
  commitment); standard Bronze 1,00 % p.a.
    - custody and FX listed as free
    - a Brokerage mode for direct ETF and stock purchases at 0,4 % / 0,3 % / 0,2
  % per order, min 39 Kč / 2 EUR / 2 USD
  - **Availability:** Specific ETFs (CSPX/VWCE) not verified.
  - **Confidence:** Medium (secondary source).

› Message from general-purpose
  ## Review of /Users/simon.slavik/clientelo-task

  I modified no files. `npm test` passes 5/5 and `npm run build` succeeds.

  ### High
  1. **The honeypot silently drops real leads.** The field is `name="company"`
  (index.html:134, 175, 208) and is checked in api/lead.js:24. Chrome and Safari
  address autofill fill "company" even with `autocomplete="off"`. A real user
  who autofills their email also fills `company`, so the server returns
  `{ok:true}` and the UI shows the report as unlocked, but no lead is saved.
  Fix: give the field a meaningless name (e.g. `hp_f7`), log every honeypot hit,
  and optionally add a minimum time-to-submit check.
  2. **The lead endpoint is an open email relay with no rate limit**
  (api/lead.js:46). Anyone can script POSTs with thousands of different
  addresses. Each one triggers a Resend email, which can be used to bomb
  inboxes, burns the Resend quota and hurts the sending domain's reputation.
  Fix: rate limit per IP with Upstash `INCR`+`EXPIRE`, check `Origin`, put a
  daily cap on sends, and consider Turnstile.
  3. **Stats are public when STATS_TOKEN is unset** (api/stats.js:9). This
  exposes the funnel, the lead count, the storage mode and whether email is
  enabled. Fix: fail closed when `VERCEL_ENV==='production'` and no token is
  set. The token also travels in the query string, so it ends up in logs and the
  Referer header.

  ### Medium
  4. **The lead count is too high in Redis mode** (api/_lib/store.js:55). `LPUSH
  leads` runs on every submit, including repeat submits, while memory mode only
  pushes new leads. Also, the `HSET` overwrites `created`, which makes the
  `HSETNX` before it pointless. Fix: count leads with `SADD`/`SCARD`, or push
  only when `isNew`, and leave `created` out of the `HSET`.
  5. **The email gate can be bypassed.** /prehled.html is public, and the full
  etfs.json (every ISIN) is in the client bundle (`dist/assets/track-*.js`). If
  the gate matters, the report has to be served after the lead is saved.
  6. **Page views are attributed to the wrong variant.**
     - prehled.html has no `data-variant`, so report.js:41 counts `page_view` as
  `twin`. Users from the fees variant who open the report get added to
  `f:twin:page_view`, and visitors arriving from the email link inflate twin
  too.
     - The variant isn't saved, so a reload without query params turns a fees
  visitor into twin.
     - Fix: store the variant in sessionStorage and send a separate
  `report_view` event from prehled.
  7. **A throwing sendBeacon skips the fallback** (src/track.js:43). If
  `sendBeacon` throws instead of returning false, the `catch` swallows the error
  and `fetch` never runs. Chrome has thrown for `application/json` Blobs in
  some versions. Fix: use a `text/plain` Blob (`readJson` already parses string
  bodies) and call `fetch` from the catch block.
  8. **No `<dialog>` fallback** (src/main.js:94). `showModal` throws a TypeError
  on iOS Safari before 15.4, so every CTA button does nothing. Fix: check for
  support and fall back to scrolling to the inline form.
  9. **Anyone can write qualify answers for any email** (api/lead.js:16-19). The
  `HSET` also creates a lead record for addresses that were never submitted.
  Fix: check the record exists first, or return a signed token from the submit
  step.
  10. **Anyone can poison the tracking data** (api/track.js). It is
  unauthenticated and accepts any `sid`. `props` and `t` are logged without size
  limits (up to 16 KB each). Fix: clip or allowlist the props.
  11. **Email links are built from the request's Host header** (api/lead.js:44).
  Use a fixed `SITE_URL` environment variable instead.

  ### Low / mobile UX (puppeteer won't catch these)
  12. **The keyboard can hide the submit button.** The sheet sits at the bottom
  with `max-height: 92vh` (src/styles.css:309). On iOS the keyboard covers the
  bottom of the screen without resizing the page, so the "Odemknout přehled"
  button can end up behind it. Fix: use `dvh`/`visualViewport`, or anchor the
  sheet to the top on small screens.
  13. **The hidden sticky CTA is still reachable** (styles.css:302-305). It is
  only moved off-screen with `translateY`, so keyboard and screen-reader users
  can still reach it. Add `inert` or `visibility:hidden` when it isn't shown.
  Its visibility also isn't recalculated when the sheet closes (main.js:190).
  14. **Malformed JSON returns 500 instead of 400.** When Vercel can't parse the
  body, its `req.body` getter throws an error that carries `statusCode`, not
  `status`, so the handlers answer 500.

  ### Checked and fine
  - **JSON import attribute:** `with { type: 'json' }` works on Node 20.10+/22,
  which Vercel uses.
  - **Request bodies:** `readJson` handles bodies Vercel has already parsed,
  including the JSON Blobs from sendBeacon.
  - **Deployment:**
    - `api/_lib` isn't exposed as a function.
    - `"type": "module"` works with the api/ functions next to the `dist`
  output.
    - The build prerenders all four pages correctly.
  - **Injection:** none.
    - The Redis REST arguments are JSON arrays.
    - Key names come only from allowlists and the validated email.
    - The email HTML contains only static data.
  - **XSS:** none via `utm_content`/`ad`. They are only compared against ticker
  names and never inserted into the page.
  - **Variant detection:** the inline script and track.js agree, because both
  read `body.dataset.variant`.
  - **Double submit:** prevented by the disabled button.

⏺ Stránka je hotová v ~/clientelo-task. Je to 9 commitov, nič som nepushol a
  repozitár zatiaľ nemá nastavený remote. Unit testy prechádzajú (5/5) a E2E
  test prejde celú cestu od príchodu po odoslanie kontaktu v oboch variantoch a
  overí, že sa započítal každý krok funnelu. Lighthouse na mobile (produkčný
  build) ukazuje výkon 98 a prístupnosť, best practices aj SEO 100.

  README a reklamy píšem pre Mateja z Clientela, ktorý bude riešenie hodnotiť.
  README je v slovenčine, stránka a reklamy v češtine.

  Čo je hotové:
  - Stránka: pri ETF z NYSE ukáže európske dvojča, ktoré si v ČR dá kúpiť (ISIN,
    poplatok, burza). Ďalej má kalkulačku „fond z banky vs. ETF“ a formulár s
    jedným poľom, ktorý odomkne celý prehľad. Po odoslaní príde nepovinná
    otázka, koľko mesačne investuje, čo zodpovedá ich kroku „Kvalifikace“.
  - Dve verzie reklamy: podľa ?ad=twin alebo ?ad=fees sa zmení nadpis aj nástroj
    na prvej obrazovke, aby nadväzovali na reklamu. Reklamy sú v ads/ s textami
    a UTM odkazmi.
  - Meranie: každý krok cesty sa ukladá podľa varianty a lievik je na
    /stats.html.
  - Dáta: sú v data/etfs.json a pri každom údaji je zdroj a dátum. Zbierali ich
    dvaja agenti a tretí ich nezávisle skontroloval. Všetkých 130 polí sedelo,
    kontrola však použila tie isté URL, nie druhý zdroj.

  Čo sa zmenilo oproti plánu:
  - Je to 12 ETF, nie 15. QQQ, VXUS a BND sa obchodujú na Nasdaqu, nie na NYSE,
    takže podľa zadania vypadli.
  - Oprava môjho staršieho vysvetlenia: strop 40 mil. Kč pri časovom teste už
    pre cenné papiere neplatí, od 1. 1. 2026 ho zrušili. Stránka uvádza aktuálny
    stav so zdrojom.
  - Zjemnil som jedno tvrdenie. Na stránke bolo „XTB ani Trading 212 vám VOO
    nepredá“, ale overené to bolo len cez stránku brokera Lynx, takže teraz tam
    je „evropský broker“.

  Ako to otestovať: dev server beží na http://localhost:5173. Skús /?ad=fees,
  /prehled.html a /stats.html. Po odoslaní e-mailu si stránka pamätá odomknutie,
  takže na opakovaný test použi anonymné okno.

  Čo urobíš ty:
  1. Nasadenie: vytvor repozitár na GitHube, pushni a nasaď na Vercel.
  2. Účty (voliteľné): bez Upstash a Resend stránka funguje, ale kontakty a
     štatistiky sa držia len v pamäti a e-mail neodchádza. Premenné sú v
     .env.example. Nastav aj SITE_URL a STATS_TOKEN, inak sú štatistiky v
     produkcii zatvorené.
  3. Právne veci: do soukromi.html treba doplniť prevádzkovateľa a nechať
     posúdiť text súhlasu aj reklamu B, ktorá menuje konkrétny fond ČSOB.
  4. Export rozhovoru: na konci spusti /export do ai-log/ a doplň do README URL
     stránky.

  README obsahuje všetko, čo zadanie vyžaduje: poradie sekcií, očakávanú
  konverziu (okolo 12 %, ide o môj odhad, nie benchmark) a tri hypotézy na A/B
  test. Je tam aj popis práce s agentmi, kde sa pomýlili, a tabuľka zo
  záverečnej kontroly kódu. Z jej 14 nálezov som 13 opravil a overil.
  Najvážnejší bol skrytý anti-bot pole s názvom company: prehliadač by ho
  vyplnil automaticky a reálne kontakty by sa potichu zahodili. Neopravený
  zostal len vedome mäkký zámok prehľadu: /prehled.html sa dá otvoriť aj bez
  e-mailu, zdôvodnenie je v README.

⏺ Auto mode lets Claude handle permission prompts automatically — Claude
  checks each tool call for risky actions and prompt injection before 
  executing. Actions Claude identifies as safe are executed, while 
  actions Claude identifies as risky are blocked and Claude may try a 
  different approach. Ideal for long-running tasks. Claude can make 
  mistakes that allow harmful commands to run, it's recommended to only 
  use in isolated environments. Shift+Tab to change mode.
